| TS_001 | EDI is able to identify the following dates in the EDI 834 file (existing functionality): Effective Date, Assessment Date, Maintenance Date. Enrollment IT can populate any of the three dates above into the transaction effective date in Facets (existing functionality). Given an inbound EDI 834 file, if the member’s eligibility span has not yet begun, the Eligibility Date (DTP*356/DTP*348) populates as the transaction effective date. | Verify AR PASSE new enrollment uses Loop 2000 Eligibility Date as transaction effective date when eligibility has not yet begun | TC_TS_001_001 | None | Positive | Functional | Validate for AR PASSE that a new inbound 834 enrollment containing Assessment Date, Effective Date, and Maintenance Date loads successfully and uses the Loop 2000 Eligibility Date as the transaction effective date in Facets when the member eligibility span has not yet started. | Applicable state and trading partner for this story are AR and AR PASSE. Prepare one unique 834 enrollment file for a brand-new member in CRT with member SSN [TEST DATA: member SSN in NM109], subscriber key [TEST DATA: subscriber SBSB_CK after load], and control number [TEST DATA: ISA13 control number]. The file must contain a future eligibility span start date of 2025-01-31 in Loop 2000 DTP*356 or DTP*348, an earlier assessment date of 2025-01-24 in Loop 2300 HD03, and a maintenance date present in Loop 2000. No existing active eligibility row for this member may begin on or before 2025-01-31 in FACPRDDB.dbo.CMC_SBEL_ELIG_ENT before execution. | 1 | Create a unique AR PASSE inbound 834 enrollment file for a new member and populate the member-level content so that NM1*IL contains SSN [TEST DATA: member SSN in NM109], Loop 2000 contains DTP*356*D8*20250131 and DTP*348*D8*20250131 as the future eligibility/effective date source, Loop 2300 HD03 contains 20250124 as the assessment date source, and the file name is unique for CRT processing. | The 834 file is prepared with AR PASSE data, the member SSN is present in NM109, the file contains 20250131 as the eligibility/effective date source, 20250124 as the assessment date source, and the file name is unique. | None |
